目前進度：首兩場票券請求表單 8/23 已送出、8/25 大會確認收到（趕在 8/24 17:00 ET 期限前；配額為首映上限 102 張、第二場 20 張，P3／P4 無配額）；8/21 兩位受邀者飯店定案（皆 Sheraton Centre Toronto，自付延住共 CAD $1,433.76 走第三方付款）、首映日用車已保留（9/17、確認號 #985636）；8/21 晚 Venue Liaison 後勤信到——四場 VL 全部指派、首映到場時間 6:40PM、14 題要儘快回覆；8/24 Sonica 的 14 題已逐題回覆——放映日公關窗口 Noah Casey、上台與紅毯名單五人、工作隨行 12 人名單已交，場館走查指定 9/17 上午；座位、車輛清單、上台順序三件仍未定；8/24 首映場夜視反盜錄已向 Securitas 下單詢價（8/25 人數定案 6 名，Securitas 原建議 7–8 名；8/25 報價到：CAD $1,464.48 含 13% 稅，要回傳簽好的合約與填妥的信用卡授權書才算訂成，最晚 9/15），早午餐人數仍待大會回覆；8/25 42West 第一封每週媒體進度信到——報導解禁時間 9/17 21:05 ET＝9/18 09:05 台灣、9/14 兩個攝影棚時段保留給導演與陳璇待我方回覆；8/25 Hollywood Reporter 確認要專訪舒華（Zoom、35–40 分鐘、要口譯，期限依信件日期推斷是 9/3）；早午餐大會改提 9/17 早餐、我方維持 9/18 11:00，卡在 9/18 第二場要不要映前介紹；8/26 大會回覆導演的證件與訂票——說明月底前寄出、兌換約 9/5–9/7；8/27 首映場 30 分鐘 Extended Tech Check 暫排 9/17 13:00、$2,200＋13% HST，要我方回覆才鎖定；8/27 大會口譯員要 screener 連結，我方回覆週末後給。
  1. 最急的一件：8/31 前把 42West 要的名單交出去——delegation list、所有要做媒體的人的 travel information 與飯店、陪同做媒體的名單（上限 2–3 位，由我方自己決定是誰）。交了 42West 才給得出第一版媒體行程表。票券表單這一關已過：8/23 送出，趕在 8/24 17:00 ET 之前；配額為首映免費 51＋付費 51（每張 CAD $48）＝上限 102 張、第二場免費 10＋付費 10（每張 $30）＝上限 20 張、P3 與 P4 沒有配額。付費票款 9/3 17:00 前付清，未認領配額 9/4 17:00 全數釋出出處：信件 ✉53✉44✉40見關鍵時程
  2. 8/21 新增：兩位受邀者飯店定案——導演與舒華都住 Sheraton Centre Toronto；舒華 9/16–20（標準房，大會特別註明非套房）、導演 9/15–20；自付延住每晚 CAD $477.92（含 6% MAT 與 13% HST），舒華 1 晚、導演 2 晚，合計 CAD $1,433.76。我方選第三方付款，授權書寄 Clifford Miu；飯店入住前 10 天扣款（導演約 9/5、舒華約 9/6），授權書沒回傳就由入住者當場自付。導演改住 Chelsea Hotel 的請求等於落空（大會逕以 Sheraton 開出訂房、全文未提 Chelsea）；房價是否含早餐已問未答出處：信件 ✉42
  3. 8/21 晚新增：Venue Liaison 後勤信到，14 題要儘快回覆——四場 VL 全部指派（P1 Sonica Carnegie、P2 Jasmina Vranesevic、P3／P4 Tahnée Chung-Lawrence）；首映到場時間 6:40PM（開演前 50 分鐘）；Royal Alexandra 沒有 green room，只有僅限 stage party 的後台等候區，隨行人員一律直接帶到座位；紅毯活動與宣傳品要事先填表核准，額外內容團隊（含社群小組）亦然。11 題現場＋3 題車輛待回，其中 entourage 名單、stage party 座位號、車輛清單卡在別處出處：信件 ✉45
  4. 8/21 新增：42West 接手媒體行程表，8/31 前給第一版——需我方先交 delegation list、所有做媒體者的 travel information 與飯店；陪同做媒體的人上限 2–3 位，且要我方自己決定是誰（Hilda 明文「不能太多人」）。42West 內部：Hilda 統籌、Kim 管首映用車，另有 Noah、Annabelle；片方信箱 Happilyeverafter@42west.com出處：信件 ✉44
  5. 8/21 首映日用車：已保留，剩 itinerary——1 台 7 人座 SUV（司機 1＋乘客 6）、最多 8 小時、只限首映日，第二場與記者會明文不提供。Global Alliance 8/21 回覆已保留 9/17 車輛，確認號 #985636；用車時段與誰坐哪台（itinerary）仍未提供，窗口移交 42West 的 Kim Gutierrez。舒華＋隨行 5 人正好坐滿 6 個位子，導演、製片、陳璇無位出處：信件 ✉41✉43

02需填表單

目前手上四份表單，按期限排。前兩份走製片線（Tiffin），後兩份走公關線（Publicist Registration，7/29 寄給 Leo）。

① 導演機票：二選一

7/27（一）前決定走哪條路。A 大會代訂：填下面這張表交回 Tiffin，大會用合作航空（法航／達美）訂票。B 自己買：免填此表，改用下方單據領 CAD $2,700 支票。出處：信件 ✉6表單原文

  • 個人資料：姓氏與名字（照護照拼字）、稱謂、性別（依護照欄位）、生日（月/日/年）、Email、電話
  • 護照：號碼、國籍、效期
  • 行程：出發與回程城市、預計去回日期（跟 7/31 要回覆 Tiffin 的抵離日期是同一組答案）、座位偏好（窗／走道／前／後）、是否與他人同行
  • 選填：航空會員號、Known Traveller Number
  • 緊急聯絡人：姓名、電話（含國碼）

直接開啟：開啟機票資料表（Google 文件）表單共享給製片的帳號，打不開就回信請 Tiffin 加開權限（她信中有提）。空白表 PDF 也在專案資料夾「要填的資料」。

選 B 自購要備的東西（換 CAD $2,700 支票）：機票電子檔（E-Ticket）、受款人姓名（須與護照一致，本人親自領取）、受款人通訊地址與 Email；受款人若不是導演本人，要另以 email 說明關係。支票在影展期間到現場領取，於大會合作銀行兌換加幣（開美金支票則無法在該行兌換）。出處：信件 ✉8

護照號碼屬敏感資料：填完直接回傳 Tiffin，不要留在共享空間。特殊需求可註記但大會不保證；建議註明避免經美國轉機。

② 證件登記表 Guest Pass Registration 已完成

7/31 已送出（製片信中確認「cast and director 的登記已完成」出處：信件 ✉13）。表內原預載兩位：阮鳳儀、葉舒華；陳璇是否在列待大會確認。✉6表單原文

  • 各自的 Email：必須是本人真實在用的信箱——會直接綁 TIFF 票務帳號，只有本人能使用，舒華這格要填藝人實際收信的信箱
  • 大頭照各一張：彩色、正面、光線充足的近照；jpg 格式、小於 1MB

直接開啟：開啟證件登記表（網址已帶存取碼）若網站另外要求輸入，存取碼

流程：Edit 逐人填、Update 暫存（可分次完成），確認全部無誤才按 Submit Final——送出後要改就得找 Tiffin。其他團隊成員的證件不走這張表：只有出席公開放映並上台 Q&A 的導演與演員能有 Guest Pass，其餘人自費買 Market Pass（registration@tiff.net）出處：信件 ✉10。不需要 Guest Pass 權益的來賓可以不填。出處：信件 ✉8

③ 公關聯絡人確認表 Publicity Contacts

8/3（一）20:00 多倫多＝台灣 8/4（二）08:00 前送出。這份表決定「媒體想採訪本片時，TIFF 把記者轉給誰」，也是 8 月中 Publicist Pass 登記的前置。出處：信件 ✉11

  • Press Office Contact：TIFF 溝通媒體事務的主要窗口
  • 至少一位地區公關：加拿大／美國／國際，三選一以上
  • 指定其中一位為 Primary Contact（主要公關）

直接開啟：開啟公關聯絡人表登入用 Filmcode 密碼

先想清楚再送：可以分次儲存，但按下送出就定案，之後改動只能寄信給 publicistregistration@tiff.net。填進去的公關聯絡方式會提供給 TIFF 認證媒體名單；沒交這份表，記者會被直接導到當初註冊表填的 Press Office Contact。若本片不聘公關，也要寄信告知由誰接媒體。

④ 試看片連結授權表 Advance Screener

同一封信附的 Google 表單，未標期限，建議與 ③ 一起處理。出處：信件 ✉11

  • 提供試看連結，或指定一位受理試看申請的聯絡人（想自己控管誰能看片就選這個）
  • 逐項勾選允許 TIFF 用於哪些用途：認證媒體、TIFF 內部研究、獎項評審、本片活動的口譯人員

直接開啟：開啟試看授權表世界首映片建議只開必要用途，並指定專人受理，避免片源外流。口譯人員若要事前看片，這格必須勾。

技術檢查（首映場專屬，自費）：首映在 Royal Alexandra Theatre，只能事先預約，兩種選項——完整試映 CAD $3,900、30 分鐘延長技術檢查 CAD $2,200（均另加 13% 稅），要儘早聯絡 dcappelletto@tiff.net。該場地不接受臨時技術檢查。Scotiabank 與 Lightbox 的場次則可免費在開場前參加標準技術檢查（限 5 分鐘，聯絡 techcheck@tiff.net）。出處：確認函

SOS現場突發狀況與通報順序›
  • 生命安全／立即危險：先打加拿大 911，再通知製片總控與 TIFF 現場人員。
  • 騷擾或不當行為：先確保當事人安全，可用 TIFF Code of Conduct 管道或 1-833-265-9835 機密通報。指南 P.11
  • 場次、紅毯、後台：Venue Liaison → Film Coordinator；不要由多人同時向不同 TIFF 部門發訊息。
  • 藝人健康或保安：藝人經紀／保鑣 → 製片總控 → Venue Liaison；社群立即暫停未發布排程。
  • DCP 技術：Steven Landry／technical@tiff.net；收到修復通知立即回覆，保留實驗室與 KDM 聯絡人待命。
  • 航班或行李：航空公司／保險 → 製片旅行負責人 → Tiffin（若影響接送、住房或必出席場次）。
  • 媒體危機：Publicist 統一事實 → 行銷與製片核准 → 必要時通知 proffice；未核准前其他人不回應。

05九大問題

劇組最常問的九個問題，點標題展開答案。

Q1需要準備什麼資料？›

宣傳素材規格出處：指南 P.4條款附錄A

  • 中＋英文劇情大綱各一（30–50 字）；導演簡歷（100 字內，含出生地、學歷、代表作）
  • 橫式劇照 3–5 張（寬高皆 ≥4000px、JPEG、彩色）——首張已交，其餘補齊
  • 導演直式照 1 張（≥4000px，須附攝影師署名）＋ 直式海報 1 張（≥4000px）
  • 預告 1 支（1080p H.264、30mb/s、5 分鐘內；另交 IG Reels 直式版 15–45 秒）
  • 片花 1–3 支（每支 2 分鐘內；已交預告則為選交）
  • PDF 版 press kit（≤2MB：英文大綱＋演員角色表＋導演簡歷）
  • 影片與導演、演員的官方社群帳號清單（IG / X / FB / TikTok）

8/10 交件進度（回覆 Tiffin 8/7 的追問）：導演直式照已交（已上傳共用雲端資料夾）；海報、預告/片花、press kit 仍在準備，press kit 由北美 PR 公司修訂中，目標 8/11 排片公告前交齊——信中用詞為「hope」，非承諾期限出處：信件 ✉21。

放映拷貝（DCP）

  • 最終版經大會後製夥伴 Deluxe 數位上傳，不必從台灣實體寄送出處：信件 ✉3條款 §V
  • 必附英文字幕（建議做成聽障友善 SDH 版）；本片為國語片，不需英文 Open Caption（信中已確認）出處：信件 ✉3
  • 規格重點：2K/4K、24/25/30fps、5.1 聲道（7.1 須另附 5.1 版）、≤250 Mbps、開頭不得有 bars/slate、檔名須照 ISDCF 命名規範；加密則需供 DKDM出處：條款附錄B

旅行證件（每位團員自理）

  • 逐人確認護照效期、國籍與加拿大身分；持外交部核發且載有身分證字號的台灣護照、搭機入境者，通常需用實際搭機的同一本護照申請 eTA。加拿大公民、永久居民、雙重國籍與其他國籍規則不同，須用官方工具逐人確認。
  • 需要簽證者：大會可出簽證支持信，加拿大移民署特殊活動代碼 26TIFF出處：邀請函
  • 需確認 eTA／訪客簽證不等於工作許可。導演、演員、製片、行銷、公關、社群攝影等工作內容不同，出發前應依加拿大商務訪客規則逐人確認是否屬 business visitor、免工作許可類別或需要其他文件；不確定時請向 TIFF／IRCC 或移民專業人士書面確認。
Q2這一屆跟往屆有何不同？›

大會文件並沒有「與往屆對照」的段落，以下是文件中可見的本屆制度，加上少量背景補充。

  • 文件明載 所有受邀者證件（Guest Pass）都含 The Market Summit 與全部 Market 場館的進場資格。補充背景 TIFF 官方內容市場「TIFF: The Market」2026 年首度全面營運，是本屆最大的新制——賣片、買家排片（Rights Survey）、Market 加場、市場區廣告都掛在它底下。出處：邀請函
  • 文件明載 登記表新增申報：是否使用生成式 AI、是否使用 Adobe 產品。出處：登記表 S4
  • 文件明載 無障礙要求明確化：非英語片必附英文字幕（鼓勵 SDH）；英語片才需要 OC/CC，與本片無關。出處：條款 §V
  • 文件明載 觀眾票選獎除主獎外，設有 International People's Choice Award（非加、非美影片專屬，本片符合資格）。出處：條款 §X
  • 補充背景 上一屆（2025）是 TIFF 50 週年的擴大慶祝屆，本屆回歸常規；冠名贊助為 Rogers。
Q3需要額外自費的部分嗎？›
大會出（文件明載）
  • 導演：機票補助 CAD $2,700（東亞出發）＋飯店 3 晚出處：導演邀請函
  • 舒華：飯店 3 晚（revised 邀請函，原版 2 晚；無書面機票補助）出處：舒華邀請函 revised 版
  • 兩位受邀者：多倫多市區機場接送、Guest Pass
  • 製片 1 名＋銷售代理 1 名：免費證件出處：指南 P.6
  • 簽證支持信、放映基本防盜錄
目前先編入自費預算
  • 目前書面招待未涵蓋者的機票、住宿（若 TIFF 後續增列招待再沖回預算）
  • 兩位受邀者超過 3 晚的延住
  • 製片、行銷、公關、統籌等所有非上台角色的證件——只能自費買 Market Pass（有折扣，折扣價未定）出處：信件 ✉10
  • DCP 製作費；逾期交片罰 CAD $350＋13% 稅出處：條款附錄B
  • Market 加場、市場區廣告、自聘公關公司出處：條款附錄A
  • 素材運費與關稅（若有實體寄送）

已去信詢問 製片 7/21 信中提出、還沒有答案的項目先別當作有或沒有——逐項與最新狀態見所有問題。

7/24–7/28 部分回覆 導演機票：大會原提代訂（法航／達美），製片 7/27 改採自購後請領 CAD $2,700 定額補助（金額由 TIFF 認定，非實報實銷；大會尚未書面確認接受此方式）。延住可協助訂房並提供房價建議；口譯與無障礙需求收集中。出處：信件 ✉6表單原文

7/31–8/3 更新 口譯已確認由大會出：公開放映的中文口譯免費提供（舒華、陳璇）；自辦活動（junket、專訪）要口譯須自費，大會推薦 Amero Communications。演員機票：7/31 改請舒華台北→多倫多單程商務艙（取代原「商務艙或 CAD $5,500」），8/1 已轉 Festival Invitation Team，Tiffin 提醒送演員機票非常罕見——預算仍按自費預留。飯店等級：原「五星」rider 已由舒華團隊降為「四星以上套房」；蔡凡熙退出、陳璇加開房請求撤回，兩人相關預算沖回。出處：信件 ✉13✉14✉15

8/7 更新 舒華加訂房晚已確認：Festival Invitation Team 同意加訂第 3 晚，附上修訂版邀請函出處：信件 ✉18。同一封信另帶出兩項未結事項（套房等級核對、阮鳳儀旅行日期），見所有問題。

8/12 更新 導演機票補助支票的領取規格：加幣支票，影展期間由受款人本人現場領取，並在大會鄰近的合作銀行兌換成加幣；也可開美金支票，但美金支票無法在合作銀行兌換。送件需備：機票 E-Ticket、受款人護照全名（須與領票人同一人）、通訊地址、Email；受款人若不是邀請函上的受邀來賓，另需以 email 說明兩人關係。Tiffin 明講會計部門處理需要時間出處：信件 ✉25。舒華第 4 晚（9/19–20）確定由片方自付——大會只核准加訂一晚；機票補助待較後階段決定，預算仍按自費預留。

預算重點：若大會不補助演員機票，台北↔多倫多長程機票＋隨行 11 人團隊的旅宿會是最大自費項目。補充背景 可查詢文化部影視局、TAICCA 文策院的國際影展參展補助（機票、行銷費），入選 TIFF 主單元通常符合申請門檻——請以當期公告為準。

Q4有多少去的名額？›

「去」本身沒有人數上限——差別在誰有大會招待、誰有證件。

  • 文件明載 官方來賓邀請函：2 位（導演阮鳳儀、葉舒華），邀請不可轉讓；但招待內容不同——導演有 CAD $2,700 機票補助＋3 晚住宿，舒華經 revised 版邀請函改為 3 晚住宿（原版 2 晚，8/7 加訂確認後改發）、仍沒有書面機票補助出處：邀請函revised 版
  • 文件明載 免費證件：製片 1 名＋銷售代理 1 名（執行製片不符資格、不可轉讓）出處：指南 P.6
  • 7/28 明確答覆 TIFF 沒有「Festival Pass」這種證件。Guest Pass 只給出席公開放映並上台 Q&A 的導演與演員，免費、不可購買，其他角色一律不能持有——製片、統籌、行銷、公關、經紀、妝髮、保鑣、攝影師都不在此列出處：信件 ✉10
  • 7/28 明確答覆 上述以外的人要證件，只有一條路：自費購買 Market Pass（有折扣，向 registration@tiff.net 購買；種類與價格見 tiff.net/themarket/attend）出處：信件 ✉10
  • Market Pass 的折扣價與可購買上限尚無答案（7/27 已問）見所有問題
  • 文件明載 紅毯、後台、Green Room 的 stage party／entourage／保安名單由 Venue Liaison 在 8 月初確認。這是送審管道，不是進場保證；妝髮、經紀、保鑣與攝影師均須列名並取得書面核准後才能依指定範圍工作出處：指南 P.5
  • 文件明載 製作與銷售團隊其他成員可用折扣價加購 Market 證件（tiff.net/industry）出處：條款附錄A

Guest Pass 內含權益

  • 6 張公開場預售票，每場最多 2 張（限本人＋1 位同行者）；每日 day-of 票每部片 1 張；rush 排隊資格出處：邀請函
  • Press & Market 場次、The Market Summit 與全部 Market 場館進場
  • 注意：自家片的票不含在內，須透過 Protocol Office 另外安排——首映上限 102 張、第二場 20 張，P3／P4 沒有配額；票務帳號綁本人，大會不受理代操作出處：邀請函 P.2✉40
Q5住在哪？›
  • 文件明載 住大會合作飯店，由大會窗口 Tiffin 代訂；文件未指明飯店名稱與星級出處：邀請函

  • 7/31 更新 舒華請求已改版：3 晚套房、四星以上＋9/19 late checkout（取代 7/27 的 Sheraton 指定套房版本；舒華團隊已同意降低慣常 rider）；陳璇、蔡凡熙的加開飯店請求已撤回，資源集中舒華。8/1 已轉 Festival Invitation Team出處：信件 ✉13✉14

  • 不可自行推定 場次已定（9/17 19:30、9/18 12:15），住宿至少要涵蓋 9/17、9/18 兩晚；舒華自報行程為 9/16 抵、9/19 晚離，8/7 已確認加訂第 3 晚。大會招待實際入住退房日期與房型仍須 Tiffin／附件書面確認。其餘團隊可先找可取消房價。

隨行與工作團隊訂房參考（tiff.net 飯店夥伴頁，2026/8/10 查）：✉18 提到的 TIFF 2026 Hotel Partners 頁已公開 25 家合作飯店的 TIFF 優惠價（先到先得、逐家售完；頁面明載 TIFF 不與任何第三方訂房代理合作）。與需求單相關的三家：Sheraton Centre $439、One King West $375、Hotel Victoria $359（CAD／晚，雄獅詢價中的兩家都在名單上，可直接比價）。整體價格帶 $299–$899，另有 5 家奢華飯店僅接受詢價；8/10 已有 4 家標示 TIFF 優惠價售完（含 InterContinental），要用優惠價宜早訂出處：信件 ✉18

Q7大會提供的協助有哪些？›
  • Film Coordinator（Tiffin Shing）：單一窗口，管註冊、素材、受邀者機票住宿、簽證支持

  • 簽證支持信＋特殊活動代碼 26TIFF；受邀者可預約多倫多機場接送（窗口是 Film Coordinator，不是用車公司）出處：邀請函。兩條線並存：機場接送是舒華邀請函所含，8/12 大會給的是一台可坐 4 人的 SUV✉25；首映日用車是另一份配額，1 台 7 人座 SUV、只限 9/17，第二場與記者會不提供，加訂車輛向 Global Alliance 自費出處：信件 ✉41
  • 公開放映中文口譯（已確認免費提供，Kaila 8/4 回覆進行安排中）：舒華、陳璇，介紹與 Q&A 都涵蓋；窗口 Kaila Wang（Interpreter Manager）interpretermanager@tiff.net。自辦活動（junket、專訪）不含，大會推薦自聘 Amero Communications（Patricia Beiger，416 831 3363，p.beiger@amero.ca）出處：信件 ✉15
  • 受邀者飯店各 3 晚（舒華原 2 晚，revised 版邀請函改為 3 晚）——8/21 定案為 Sheraton Centre Toronto，超出 3 晚的天數自費✉42；導演機票補助 CAD $2,700
  • 放映基本防盜錄措施（加強版付費）；無障礙技術支援出處：條款 §XI
  • 行銷公關面的協助見 Q8
Q8大會行銷、公關的資源和資訊›

大會給的曝光（文件明載）

  • 影片專頁已於 7/20 上線；節目手冊（印刷）收錄劇組名單——credits 7/23 曾確認一版、8/5 提修訂，8/7 已定案並回覆 Tiffin，趕在送印前完成：最終版含 Shuhua Yeh（無連字號）、Splash Pictures, Inc.（印刷版）／Splash Pictures Inc.（tiff.net 網頁版，因系統限制無逗號）；對外引用名單以此最終版為準出處：信件 ✉3✉17
  • 預告會上 TIFF 官方影音頻道＋King Street 數位看板（前面可能加掛 TIFF logo，影展後續留）出處：條款 §IX(5)
  • 片花提供媒體做電視、電台、網路報導
  • 海報最多印製 3 張於 TIFF 場館展示出處：條款 §IX(5)
  • 官方社群 2.5M 受眾：hashtag #TIFF26、帳號 @tiff_net——把全劇組 IG 帳號給大會，官方會轉發被 tag 的貼文出處：指南 P.10

公關系統怎麼運作

  • 公關聯絡人確認表（8/3 截止）是媒體找上門的分流開關：填了誰，TIFF 就把記者轉給誰，名單也會併入 TIFF 認證媒體名單；沒填就一律導到當初註冊表的 Press Office Contact出處：信件 ✉11
  • 試看片連結授權表決定認證媒體、獎項評審、口譯人員能否事前看片——想爭取影評與獎項聲量就要開，想控管片源就指定專人受理出處：信件 ✉11
  • 聘公關公司非必要但大會建議；大會提供歷屆合作的 Publicist Directory 名單（Google 試算表，出自 Filmmaker Guide）出處：指南 P.5
  • 8/10 確認 片方北美公關已拍板由 42West 合作，詳細行程規劃仍在討論中（此為片方與 TAICCA 往來信件中揭露，非大會資訊）出處：信件 ✉23
  • PR 聯絡資料的公開日不等於補交截止；實際期限仍待 TIFF 確認。Publicist Pass 由大會另行通知，名額有限且不可轉讓出處：指南 P.6
  • Publicist Pass 給工作進場權：自家片的 P&M 場與紅毯
  • 媒體相關問題：proffice@tiff.net

賣片與市場（自費項目）

  • Rights Survey 會影響買家是否優先看片
  • 可自費購買 Market 私人放映與場館廣告；期限見關鍵時程
Q9其他注意事項›
  • 世界首映義務：9/17 前不得在任何地方（含線上）公開放映；影展結束（9/20）前不得在加拿大商業放映或上網，違反可能被取消資格出處：影片邀請函條款 §III
  • 交給大會的素材與 DCP 都視同最終版；交件後原則上不收替換版出處：條款 §IX附錄B
  • 片長、規格若有變動要立刻通知大會，否則可能失去場次出處：條款附錄A
  • 所有參加者受 TIFF 行為準則與票務條款約束，違反可撤證
  • People's Choice 參賽在註冊表勾選；得獎影響大（見下方案例）出處：登記表 S7
  • 公開場一律對號入座，公關不得替來賓換位；開演前 15 分鐘未入座，保留席會被釋出出處：指南 P.7條款 §VIII

06所有問題

07角色分工

給分配工作用：每個角色點開自己的段落，就看到跟自己有關的事。帶「出處」標籤的來自大會文件，排在前面優先處理；其餘是內部提醒，排在後面。

全員共同規則›

文件相關（優先）

  • 大會信件統一由製片 Linhan 收發（大會邀請函的收件人），收到大會信先轉給他出處：影片邀請函
  • 所有會上台的人（介紹、Q&A、紅毯）先知情：TIFF 會錄影且永久使用出處：條款 §VII
  • 票務帳號綁本人，不能請別人代管出處：邀請函 P.2
  • 只有上台 Q&A 的導演與演員能拿 Guest Pass，其他人（製片、行銷、公關、經紀、妝髮、保鑣）一律自費買 Market Pass出處：信件 ✉10

內部提醒

  • 護照號碼、證件影本、私人電話、藝人動線只放受控文件，不進公開或共享頁面

使用方式：表內時刻只供規劃方向，不代表 9 月每一天都有同一班次或完全相同時刻。開票當天須在長榮官網輸入實際日期，重新確認班號、起降時間、行李、改票／退票條件與團體訂位。回程若是凌晨航班，集合與退房日應按「前一晚」安排。

導演機票二選一：大會經合作航空（法航／達美）代訂（7/27 前填 Traveller Profile Form），或自己買再憑 E-Ticket 等單據領 CAD $2,700 支票（影展期間現場領）。填表時建議註明避免經美國轉機，否則需另辦美國過境文件。本章的直飛建議適用其他自費成員。出處：信件 ✉6表單原文

舒華 7/31 回報大會的行程：9/16 抵、9/19 晚間離出處：信件 ✉13；需求單為 BR36 9/16 晚 21:20 抵，首映前只有一夜調時差（原建議提早 2–3 天）——這是藝人團隊自行決定的日期，妝髮與紅毯時程要按「首映日狀態未必最佳」預留緩衝。需求單的退房日為 9/20 且回程航班未列，與 7/31 版不一致，見所有問題；去程若由大會出單程商務艙（請求中），回程另行自訂。

與大會招待對齊：招待承諾導演最多 3 晚、舒華 3 晚（8/7 加訂確認），確切入住／退房日尚未確認；先找可取消房價，收到 Tiffin 書面確認再定案（出處：邀請函）。導演機票應先問清 CAD $2,700 的訂票／核銷方式。入境文件必須逐人依護照與工作內容判斷，不可用「全員辦 eTA」一概處理。

備案航線（若直飛滿位或票價過高）：經溫哥華（長榮/華航直飛 YVR 轉加國內線，入境手續在 YVR 辦）、經香港（國泰）、經東京（加航直飛 YYZ）。避免經美國轉機——需另辦美國過境簽證/ESTA、行李提領重掛，大團隊風險高。

09參考案例與類比

補充背景 本節全部來自大會文件以外的一般背景知識，細節請以 TIFF 官網節目資料庫查證後再對外引用。

  • Special Presentations 的定位：TIFF 沒有公布 Gala 與 Special Presentations 的正式高低排序；實務上 SP 是明星、名導與話題片集中的重要公開單元。歷屆片目可作溝通背景，但不應寫成官方「僅次於 Gala」的保證。本片以世界首映進 SP，對國際媒體與買家仍是清楚且有價值的訊號。
  • 首映場地的意義：暫定首映場 Royal Alexandra Theatre 是千人級歷史劇院，SP 單元的指標場地之一，通常伴隨媒體拍照區；確切紅毯規格等 8 月大會通知。
  • People's Choice 的價值：TIFF 同時設有 Platform、加拿大電影與 Short Cuts 等評審獎，不能說「沒有評審團大獎」。People's Choice 是最具國際能見度的觀眾票選獎之一；國際片另設 International People's Choice，本片依 2026 條款符合資格。任何投票動員都應先確認大會規則，避免不當誘因或票券促銷。
  • 台灣團隊常見做法：入選國際 A 級影展主單元後，向文化部影視局／TAICCA 申請參展補助（機票、國際行銷費），並由國內發行方同步操作「入選 TIFF」新聞稿與 9 月首映的二波宣傳。

配額（Allocations）——Happily Ever After

  • 1st Public Screening｜Date: 9/17/2026；Time: 7:30 PM；Venue: RA（Royal Alexandra Theatre）
    • Complimentary ticket allocation: up to 51
    • Paid ticket allocation available: up to 51；Ticket price: $48
    • Total ticket allotment (complimentary + purchasable): up to 102
  • 2nd Public Screening｜Date: 9/18/2026；Time: 12:15 PM；Venue: SB3（Scotiabank 3）
    • Complimentary ticket allocation: up to 10
    • Paid ticket allocation available: up to 10；Ticket Price: $30.00
    • Total Ticket Allotment (complimentary + purchasable): up to 20

Please note: All prices are in Canadian dollars and include tax + Ticketmaster service fees.

Key Dates

  • Deadline to submit ticket requests via form: August 24th, 2026（信首另明載 no later than Monday, August 24th at 5 PM ET）
  • Deadline to complete the payment on purchased allocated tickets: 5pm on Thursday, September 3rd, 2026
  • All allocations released to public sale: September 4th, 2026（信中另述 all unclaimed ticket allocations will be released at 5pm ET on September 4th）

Protocol Office Policies（節錄原文）：These tickets must accommodate the needs of all involved stakeholders including directors, sales agents, distributors, producers, cast, crew and other parties involved with the film. Every person sitting through the film will require a ticket.

Policies（續）：Primary rights holder status is assigned in the following descending order: Canadian Distributor；US Distributor and/or Studio；International Sales Agent；Domestic US Sales Agent；Producers and Production Company.

Policies（續）：Feature films receive an allocation of tickets to only the first and second Public screenings. … The purchasable allocation is designed to enable film teams to access an inventory of tickets prior to our Public on-sale. Should the film's stakeholders need additional tickets outside of the allocation provided, they are welcome to purchase tickets via Public sale. If any members of your film team are accredited, they may attempt to claim advance or same day tickets to the screening depending on their pass type, subject to availability. … Press & Market screenings are not ticketed as they are accessible via Market accreditation.

Requesting Tickets：Please note that requests for ticket numbers above and beyond what is listed below will be denied. Please be aware that if no Public Ticket Allocation response is received by August 24th at 5pm ET, a portion of your allocation may be released to public sales.

Returning Tickets：Tickets that are no longer needed from your allocation may be returned to the Protocol Office by emailing protocol@tiff.net. Once tickets have been transferred to another account, they are no longer eligible for return processing. Tickets from the paid allocation are non-refundable. Unused tickets should be returned, at minimum, 4 days prior to the screening.

Next Steps：Following your request, a member from our team will send confirmation that includes instructions on how to access and manage your ticket allocation. In the event that you have requested purchasable tickets, details on how to pay your invoice via our Account Manager will be shared.

〔按：本片票券配額的唯一權威數字，此前所有「首兩場有免費＋付費配額但沒有數字」的敘述到此為止。四項要點：①兩場配額落差極大——首映合計上限 102 張（免費 51＋付費 51），第二場合計上限僅 20 張（免費 10＋付費 10）。對照我方 ✉36 提報的需求（首映 40、第二場 20），首映有餘裕，第二場的 20 張需求正好用滿全部配額、不留餘量；P3、P4 各 20 張的需求則完全沒有配額可用（✉39 已明文）。②付費票要花錢：首映每張 CAD $48、第二場每張 $30（均含稅與 Ticketmaster 手續費），若把付費配額全部認領，首映 51 張約 CAD $2,448、第二場 10 張 $300。③三個死線：8/24 17:00 ET（＝台灣 8/25 05:00）前以表單提出請求，逾時部分配額直接釋出公售；9/3 17:00 前付清付費票款；9/4 17:00 未認領配額全數釋出。付費票不可退，用不到的票至少要在放映前 4 天退回。④超額請求會被直接駁回，配額上限沒有商量空間；不足的部分只能等公開售票自行購買，或由持證者用證件本身的預售／day-of 權益碰運氣（不保證有票）。附件 Protocol Ticket Policies.pdf 尚未存入「TIFF 大會文件」資料夾——需由收信人自信箱另存；本節政策原文取自信件內文，內容與附件同名章節一致。〕

P.1 · 出席條件、接待、證件

TIFF invites Shuhua to participate in the 51st Toronto International Film Festival with the film HAPPILY EVER AFTER. Please note that this guest invitation is non-transferable.

SCREENING ATTENDANCE — The following offer is dependent on the Guest attending the Festival, at minimum, for the first two (2) public screenings of the Film, for both the introduction and question-and-answer sessions. Introductions, Q&A sessions, and any additional official Festival engagements may be recorded and made available on the Festival's online platforms and/or social media. By late July, you will be informed via email of your confirmed screening dates and times.

HOSPITALITY — It is the responsibility of those who travel to Canada to ensure they meet travel documentation requirements. For visa support, the Immigration, Refugees and Citizenship Canada Special Event Code for this event is: 26TIFF.

Airport transfers: TIFF can provide airport transfers within Toronto. These can be booked up to five (5) days prior to arrival.

Accommodations: TIFF is pleased to offer up to three (3) nights of accommodation at one of our Hospitality Partners. This booking must be made through your Film Coordinator. Should you wish to extend your stay, we would be pleased to help you arrange additional hotel nights based on the hotel's availability.

版本註：原版（7 月）此段為 up to two (2) nights；8/7 大會確認加訂第 3 晚後改發 revised 版，逐字比對唯一改動即此處 2→3 晚，其餘一字未動。兩版 PDF 均存「TIFF 大會文件」資料夾出處：信件 ✉18

ACCREDITATION (GUEST PASS) — A Guest Pass will be provided for each invited guest: six (6) advance tickets to Public screenings (max two per screening, for the Guest Pass holder and one additional person); first-come, first-served access to select Press & Market screenings; access to The Market Summit and all Market venues; day-of tickets (up to 1 per film) daily; Rush ticket access.

P.2 · 票務、行為準則

This Guest Pass will be linked to a Ticketmaster account created by TIFF. For security reasons, only the named Guest Pass holder can receive support from TIFF Customer Relations. No one else will be able to access ticketing benefits on behalf of the named Guest Pass holder.

Guest Passes do not guarantee access to your own Film's screenings. Tickets to your own screenings must be secured through TIFF's Protocol Office (protocol@tiff.net). The Protocol office begins organizing tickets to individual films in early August.

SAFETY AND RESPECT — By participating in the Festival you agree to abide by TIFF's Code of Conduct. Passes and tickets may be revoked should the Code of Conduct be breached.

Your Film Coordinator will be in touch to introduce themselves and help facilitate your Festival participation. — Sonia Sakamoto-Jog, Senior Director, Festival Programming & Guest Services

Filmmaker Guide 參展手冊TIFF 2026 Filmmaker Guide · 11 頁›
P.3 · Timeline 時間表
  • Film Invitation Acceptance — invitation sent in May/June/July; acceptance due as soon as possible. 窗口：Film Coordinator
  • Publicity Materials Delivery — due within 48 hours of invitation. 窗口：Film Coordinator
  • Film Announcement — June to August（本片 7/20）
  • Accreditation — July。Director and Cast: Film Coordinator；Production and Sales Team: registration@tiff.net；Publicist: publicistregistration@tiff.net
  • Confirmed Film Schedule — Late July（schedules@tiff.net）
  • Travel and Accommodations — Early August, after the film schedule is locked。窗口：Film Coordinator
  • Screening and Venue Logistics — Early August。窗口：Venue Liaison
  • Programming Questions — Ongoing。窗口：your film's Programmer
P.4 · Film Publicity Materials 宣傳素材清單

Materials should be sent directly to your Film Coordinator within 48 hours of your film's official invitation. Feature films:

  • 3–5 colour horizontal film stills (minimum width/height of 4000 pixels)
  • 1 vertical director photo (minimum 4000 pixels), with photo credit
  • 1 digital press kit (including, at minimum, a synopsis and cast and character list)
  • 1 digital film poster (minimum 4000 pixels)
  • 1 compressed film trailer, not exceeding 5 minutes
  • 3–5 film clips

Trailers and clips: minimum 1080p H.264 Quicktime at 30mb/s or higher with AAC 320kb/s audio. Whenever possible, please provide ProRes Masters (422 or better) with 5.1 and split audio for in-cinema and editorial use. Deliver via download link (e.g. WeTransfer).

P.5 · Before the Festival（版權調查、公關、場地）

Film Rights Survey — The film owners must inform the Market team about the available territories and rights-holder contact information for all sold territories by July 24, 2026. Available rights information will be published in our Rights Availability List and will allow buyers to prioritise their screening schedule.

Press Announcements — Your film's participation is strictly confidential until officially announced by TIFF. Once announced, visible at tiff.net. Questions: proffice@tiff.net.

Hiring a Publicist? — Hiring an agency or publicist is not required but will ensure you have dedicated resources pitching your film individually to the media. Linked: Publicist Directory for TIFF 2026 Filmmakers（歷屆合作公關名單）.

Scheduling — Upon receipt of your Tentative Schedule Letter, you will have 48 hours to submit schedule-related change requests to schedules@tiff.net. Requested changes are not guaranteed.

Screening and Venue Logistics — By early-August, your Film Coordinator will introduce you to the Venue Liaisons assigned to your film's screenings, to confirm: arrival times and locations; stage party and entourage; Green Room list; security; sampling and activations; all information related to Red Carpet events (if applicable).

P.6 · Accreditation 證件

Director and Talent Accreditation — Each director and cast who receives an Official Guest Invitation is eligible for a complimentary Guest Pass（權益同邀請函所列）.

Production and Sales Team Accreditation — Each film in Official Selection is eligible for one complimentary pass for a producer on the film and one complimentary pass for a sales agent representing the film. These passes are non-transferable. Executive producers are ineligible. Registration coordinated through the TIFF Protocol team (protocol@tiff.net).

Publicist Registration — Publicists representing the film will need to register for a credential. In late August, TIFF will contact the film's main Publicity Contacts to register publicists for Publicist Passes. The Publicist Pass provides working access to the film's Festival events including the film's Press & Market screening(s) and Red Carpet, if applicable. Note: there is a limit of Publicist Passes per film; non-transferable. Talents' personal publicists will also need to register. A valid ticket is required to take a seat in the cinema.

P.7 · Tickets 自家片票券

Tickets to your own film's public screenings are coordinated through the primary rights holder's Protocol Coordinator (protocol@tiff.net). The Protocol team begins organizing tickets for film teams in early August. All tickets can be accessed and managed digitally as early as August 15.

All film team members who plan to attend their film's public screenings, including the director and any attending cast, must secure their tickets through the Protocol Office.

Note: All seating for public screenings within Festival venues is assigned. Guests must remain in the seat indicated on their ticket. Publicists and their representatives are not permitted to relocate guests from their assigned seats.

P.8 · Accessibility Requirements 無障礙要求

All non-English-language films will be required to include English subtitles and we strongly encourage these productions to use English subtitles for the D/deaf and hard of hearing (SDH).

Exhibition copies of English-language films in the Gala and Special Presentations sections must include a version with open captions (OC) in English.（英語片才適用，本片為國語片）

P.9 · 字幕製作注意

Closed captions must not exceed 32 characters per line, 3 lines limit. Opt for human instead of AI captioning to improve accuracy. Audio description mixing: track 8 of your DCP, description only. 技術問題：accessibility@tiff.net。

P.10 · Connect with the Festival 社群

TIFF has a global audience of over 2.5 million film lovers across Instagram, TikTok, YouTube, X/Twitter, Facebook, and Letterboxd. Join the conversation online using #TIFF26 — the only official hashtag of the 51st edition.

If your film has an official Instagram, X/Twitter, or TikTok account, please share this information with your Film Coordinator. We would also appreciate a list of Instagram handles for your film's team, distributors, studio, etc. Tag us in any Festival-related posts, use the hashtag, and keep an eye out to reshare any posts you're tagged in. 帳號：@TIFF_NET（IG/TikTok/X）、@TIFF_INDUSTRY（X）。

P.11 · Safety and Respect

TIFF does not tolerate violence, mistreatment, or harassment. Confidentially report wrongdoing: tiff.net/code-of-conduct；toll-free 1-833-265-9835；Emergencies: call 911.

2026 參展條款 Terms & ConditionsFestival Terms And Conditions · 17 頁›
P.1 · §I–IV 適用範圍、註冊、參展協議、單元

§II Film Registration — In order to accept the Festival's invitation, an individual having authority to represent the Film must complete and submit the online Film Registration Form at festivalinvitations.tiff.net.

§III(3) — Participating Films must not be screened commercially in Canada or made available on the internet in Canada, prior to September 20, 2026, except with TIFF's prior written consent.

§IV Programmes — Centrepiece, Discovery, Gala Presentations, Midnight Madness, Platform, Primetime, Short Cuts, Special Presentations, TIFF Classics, TIFF Docs, Wavelengths.

P.2 · §V 放映拷貝

§V(1) — The Registrant must supply TIFF with an exhibition copy of the Film by August 21, 2026 in the Theatrical Screening Format. Failure to deliver by deadline may result in administrative fees payable to TIFF.

§V(2)a — All non-English-language Films will be required to include English subtitles; SDH strongly encouraged.

§V(2)b — OC 要求僅限英語片（Gala/SP）。

§V(4) Inbound Digital Delivery (Features) — Please contact Steven Landry, slandry@tiff.net, to make arrangements to receive a secure upload link through our post-production partner, Deluxe.

P.3 · §VI 排片、§VII 放映出席與錄影授權

§VI(1) — TIFF may schedule Films for up to five (5) in-person screenings inclusive for the public and Press & Market audiences. The Registrant will receive at least two (2) public screenings and one Press & Market screening. The time(s), date(s), and locations of all screenings will be selected by TIFF in its sole discretion and are subject to change at any time.

§VI(4) — Tentative schedule letter from schedules@tiff.net no later than July 20, upon which change requests within 48 hours. Confirmed Schedule Letter no later than July 27, 2026. No more change requests accepted after the Confirmed Schedule Letter is sent.

§VII(1) — TIFF requires that the Film's director, and any other guests participating in the Festival with the Film, will be present, at minimum, for the first two (2) public screenings of the Film, including press/media activities, an introduction and a Q&A session. For Films with a red carpet or photo call, TIFF will provide further logistical details in August 2026.

§VII(2) — TIFF and its partners may record all Festival events (publicity events, introductions, Q&A, press conferences…). Participants grant to TIFF irrevocable permission to use the Recordings in perpetuity, in any manner and in all media, for any purpose connected to TIFF's charitable mandate.

P.4 · §VII(4) 首映交通、§VIII 票務、§IX 素材

§VII(3) — The Film will be presented at an in-person venue.

§VII(4) — Guests and directors of all Films in the Gala Presentations programme and some Films in the Special Presentations programme may be offered transportation to the first public screening of the Film. TIFF will provide further logistical details in August 2026.

§VIII(2) — Market passes, Guest Passes and tickets may not be resold or used for fundraising, promotions, contests, prizes or giveaways without TIFF's prior written consent.

§VIII(3) — Market passes and tickets are non-refundable. Tickets may be exchanged starting September 8. Complimentary day-of tickets may be redeemed daily at 8am starting September 10.

§VIII(7) — TIFF Customer Relations requires government-issued photo ID for in-person support. TIFF does not permit access to accounts on behalf of another person.

§IX(2) — The Registrant is responsible for arranging and paying for the shipping of materials. Any shipping charges incurred by TIFF will be billed back to the Registrant.

§IX(4) — TIFF considers all materials sent by Registrants to be final and ready for publication.

P.5 · §IX(5) 素材使用、§X 獎項

§IX(5) — The Registrant warrants that it possesses all rights needed to provide the Promotional Materials for TIFF's permitted uses and agrees to indemnify TIFF for claims arising from their use.

§IX(5)a — TIFF may display all Film trailers on the TIFF digital signage system, TIFF online video and social channels, at digital publicity events and in cinemas, and may place a TIFF or Festival logo before all trailers. All trailers will remain on TIFF online video channels after the Festival, unless a removal request is emailed to materials@tiff.net.

§IX(5)c — TIFF may print and mount up to a maximum of three (3) posters to be displayed at TIFF's premises, retained in TIFF's archives, gifted to TIFF sponsors, or auctioned to raise funds.

§X(1) People's Choice Award Presented by Rogers — winner selected by a public voting mechanism administered by TIFF. The winner must make the winning Film available to TIFF upon request to be screened for public audiences on the final day of the Festival（含一場付費的志工專場）.

§X(3) — The winning filmmaker(s) of TIFF's awards must submit a video message to be shared on TIFF's social media channels and to be potentially played on-screen at an in-person awards ceremony.

P.6 · §X(4) 獎項清單
  • People's Choice Award — all feature films/series with a 2026 premiere date
  • People's Choice Documentary / Midnight Madness Awards
  • International People's Choice Award — all international (non-Canadian, non-US) feature films with a 2026 premiere date. The film's language is not a consideration for eligibility.（本片符合資格）
  • Platform Award、Best Canadian Feature、Best Canadian Discovery、Short Cuts ×3（本片不適用加拿大類）
P.7 · §XI 責任與授權、防盜錄

§XI(1) — The Film Owners warrant that the Film is original or that all required consents, releases, licences and waivers have been obtained for every person, location, trademark, sound recording, musical composition and other protected work included in the Film.

§XI(3) — TIFF offers basic anti-piracy measures at each screening. Studios, distributors, sales agents and production companies may augment, on a fee-for-service basis, enhanced coverage where trained personnel employ proven industry tools and techniques. Contact your Venue Liaison. While in TIFF's possession, all Films are securely stored.

P.10–11 · 附錄A 素材交件表（前段）

It is of vital importance that you notify us immediately if there are any changes to the screening format or run time, as this has scheduling implications that may result in the loss of screenings.

  • Online Registration Form — 48 hours after receiving the Invitation Package
  • 30–50 word synopsis（英文＋原文）— 48 hours
  • Director biography and filmography — max 100 words — 48 hours
  • 3–5 horizontal stills — ≥4000px JPEG colour — 48 hours
  • 1 vertical director photo — ≥4000px + photo credit — 48 hours
  • 1 trailer — 1080p H.264 30mb/s, AAC 320kb/s，交下載連結 — 48 hours
  • 1 PDF press kit — 英文大綱＋cast & character list＋導演簡歷，不超過 2MB — 48 hours
  • 1–3 broadcast-quality clips — 每支 ≤2 分鐘（已交預告則選交）— 48 hours
  • 1 vertical digital poster — ≥4000px JPEG — 48 hours
  • Official Social Media Handles — IG/X/FB/TikTok — 48 hours
  • 1 Instagram Reels 版預告 — 直式 1.91:1–9:16、≥30FPS、720px、15–45 秒 — 48 hours
P.12–13 · 附錄A 交件表（後段）
  • Exhibition Copy — August 21, 2026（slandry@tiff.net）
  • Film Public Screening scheduling requests — July 13* (schedules@tiff.net) *邀請晚於 7/10 者於受邀時提出
  • Film Rights Survey — July 24, 2026
  • Ticket allotment — August 12, 2026 (protocol@tiff.net)：Each Film is entitled to an allotment of paid and complimentary tickets for the Film's first and second in-person public screenings. Resale not permitted. TIFF: The Market will be in touch with the Film's primary rights holder by mid-August.
  • Complimentary passes for Feature Films — August 17, 2026 (registration@tiff.net)：one pass for a producer + one pass for a sales agent；non-transferable；executive producers ineligible。Other members of the production and sales agency can purchase a pass at a discounted rate at tiff.net/themarket/attend.
  • Advertising opportunities — August 6, 2026 (themarketsales@tiff.net)：early booking window for promotional advertising throughout Market venues including King Street.
  • Market screenings — August 14, 2026 (themarketscreenings@tiff.net)：early-booking window to purchase Market screening slots or additional private screenings. First-come, first-served.
P.14–15 · 附錄B DCP 技術規格

Deliver your Exhibition Copy on or before August 21, 2026. Physical deliveries made directly to venues will not be accepted.

  • 2K or 4K resolution；single CPL；jpeg2000/mxf Interop or SMPTE；24, 25, 30 fps only
  • Subtitles burned into the image are the most reliable method and are preferred
  • Audio: 5.1（Stereo 不建議）；24-bit 48KHz；7.1 須另附 5.1 版
  • Max bit rate 250 Mbps（建議編碼上限設 245）；VBR preferred
  • Encryption is optional；檔名須照 ISDCF 命名規範（isdcf.com/dcnc），Content Title Text 不符會被退件
  • 開頭不得出現 bars、tones、slates、countdown、廣告或預告，違者退件
  • Container 尺寸須符合長寬比：1998×1080 (1.85) / 2048×858 (2.39) / 1920×1080 (16:9)
P.16–17 · 附錄B 加密、逾期罰款、替換版

KDM/Encryption — If encrypted, supply a DKDM. Server certificates and DKDM information provided no later than August 8, 2026. Festival keys valid September 10–20.

Late-Delivery Penalties — Content providers delivering later than August 21 or failing to provide a DKDM will be billed an administrative fee of CAD $350 (plus 13% HST).

Replacement Versions — TIFF is not obligated to accept replacement versions after the delivery deadline. The first version we receive will be considered the final version and will be used for all Festival screenings unless a technical issue is identified.

Technical questions: technical@tiff.net.

Section 1 · Film Details

片名（英文＋原文）、語言、英文字幕有無、紀錄片/動畫/闔家觀賞、英文＋原文大綱（30–50 字）、是否導演首部劇情長片、製作年份、出品國、院線上映日、線上曝光紀錄、參加過的影展與獎項、Premiere Status（World Premiere…）、官網與社群帳號。

Section 2 · Production Teams

主製作公司與聯絡人、共同製作公司、監製、執行監製、國際銷售代理、美國銷售代理、加拿大發行商、美國發行商、國家電影推廣機構——各含公司名、Email、電話。

Section 3 · Film Credits

原著改編資訊、導演（代名詞、行動需求、是否需要口譯及語言、自我認同問題、簡歷）、Director Liaison（導演聯絡窗口）、編劇、攝影、剪接、美術、造型、配樂、聲音、主要演員。

Section 4 · Technical Details

彩色/黑白、片長（分鐘）、放映格式（DCP/3D/4K）、聲音格式（2.0/5.1/7.1）、KDM 加密與聯絡人、拷貝寄送來源。Have you used Generative Artificial Intelligence (AI) in the creation of this production?（Yes 需說明）、Open Captions 聯絡意願、Have you used any Adobe products?

Section 5–6 · Exhibition Copy 條件、Publicity 公關窗口

同意放映拷貝條件。Publicity：Press Office 聯絡公司與聯絡人（必填）、加拿大/美國/國際 Publicist 公司與聯絡人、Primary Publicist 指定。

Section 7 · Final Agreements

If your film is eligible, do you agree to participate in the People's Choice Award presented by Rogers?；獎項資訊揭露同意（姓名、性別、族裔用於獎項資格判定與多元共融統計）；協議簽署公司與聯絡人。

來賓事件處理與行為準則TIFF 2026 Visitor Protocol & CoC · 2 頁 · ✉45 附件›
P.1 · Visitor Incident Protocol／Code of Conduct

TIFF VISITOR INCIDENT PROTOCOL — A cornerstone of TIFF's belief system is that everyone has the right to respect, as well as fair and equitable treatment from others. Our staff and volunteers are committed to ensuring the best possible experience for all of our attendees. If the safety of our guests, staff or volunteers are in question, we will take immediate steps to ensure the matter is addressed. The Code of Conduct is intended to maintain and protect TIFF's integrity, its resources and its organizational processes.

TIFF VISITOR CODE OF CONDUCT — TIFF is committed to providing and maintaining an environment in which all visitors, staff and volunteers are free from workplace violence, harassment, sexual harassment and discrimination based on their race, ancestry, place of origin, colour, ethnic origin/identity, disability, citizenship, creed, sex (including pregnancy), sexual orientation, gender identity, age, marital status, family status, receipt of public assistance, record of offences, political affiliation, religion affiliation, language and/or socio-economic status. All visitors are responsible for conducting themselves in a manner that promotes a productive and positive environment and exhibits respect for all individuals, including in their dealings with TIFF staff, volunteers, stakeholders, partners, clients, suppliers or the public.

EXAMPLES OF "UNACCEPTABLE BEHAVIOUR" ARE, BUT NOT LIMITED TO: Physically or psychologically aggressive behaviour；Threats of violence, whether oral or written；Intimidating, abusive or bullying behaviour that causes the recipient to have fear of physical violence.

P.2 · 違反的後果與適用對象

CONSEQUENCES OF VIOLATING THE CODE OF CONDUCT — Any individual covered by this Policy that is found to have violated this Policy in respect of harassment, discrimination, workplace violence or retaliation will be subject to immediate and appropriate disciplinary action, including but not limited to warnings, reprimands, suspensions, cancellation of tickets/passes, or bans.

Visitors include: TIFF Members, Ticket Holders, TIFF Board Members, Industry, Guests (Talent/Filmmakers), Press, Third Party Clients, and TIFF Partners. Visitors that break TIFF's Code of Conduct will be contacted by TIFF Management who will communicate the appropriate consequences for the breach of conduct. For more information：tiff.net/code-of-conduct

〔按：這份是規範，不含要我方填答或決定的事項。對本片唯一實質的一點是適用對象明列 Guests（Talent/Filmmakers）——導演、演員與隨行同樣受這份準則拘束，罰則到取消票證與禁入為止。信中另給的 tiff.net/code-of-conduct 是同一份準則的公開版。〕

安全資訊表TIFF 2026 - Security Info Sheet · 4 頁 · ✉45 附件 · 大會註明機密›
P.1 · 大會提供的人群管制與安全措施

All security related activities are coordinated by the Festival Security office, responsible for planning and logistics in three areas：Crowd Control and Safety、Protest/Demonstration Protocol、Anti-Piracy.

Crowd Control and Safety measures provided by TIFF at each screening：

  • Venue walk-throughs provided jointly by the Venue Liaisons and Security teams. These walk-throughs clearly outline talent arrival, holding, and exit procedures including routes through the venues
  • Licensed security personnel, where necessary, to monitor the red carpet events
  • Security screening and access controls, including QR coded tickets, bag checks and scanner gates
  • Coordination with existing facility security at all screening venues
  • Coordination with police enforcement and paid duty police officers, where necessary, for crowd and traffic control, including road closures, vehicle mitigation measures and infrastructure

Please note（原文）：The Festival does not coordinate or provide close protection services to travel with or accompany talent, VIPs, and guests. Should you require such services, please ask your Venue Liaison for information regarding local and licensed service providers.

As stated in the Terms & Conditions and notwithstanding this Security Policy, TIFF bears no responsibility in the event of theft, unauthorized access or copying, or any breach of copyright of films screened as part of the Festival.

P.2 · 抗議與示威協定

TIFF will manage protests by prioritizing an approach of acknowledgement and de-escalation as the first response. This strategy emphasizes allowing peaceful demonstrations and protest activity to occur.

If peaceful protests begin to disrupt operations or scheduled activities, TIFF is prepared to implement pre-planned operational alternatives：Increased security coverage from private suppliers and Toronto Police Services；Alternate red carpet locations in the event transportation routes cannot be secured and/or primary carpet setups are not able to be used；Enhanced crowd control measures around high profile areas.

If protest activities contravene the law or pose risks to safety, TIFF will request law enforcement assistance to disperse protesters. This will be the last resort.

P.3 · 反盜錄：基本措施與付費服務費率

Standard anti-piracy measures for Festival 2026 include：Anti-Piracy statements in informational materials including the TIFF website and venue signage；Anti-piracy messaging within pre-shows and film trailers at the beginning of Festival screenings；Trained venue ushers who immediately report suspicious behavior to Festival staff and security；Secure storage and regular inventory of copyrighted materials while on Festival premises.

Specialized Anti-Piracy Services on Fee-for-Service Basis — Specialized anti-piracy services at indoor screening venues can be arranged on a fee-for-service basis. For Festival 2026, these anti-piracy services are exclusively provided by Securitas. Securitas personnel are solely authorized to provide these specialized services. Services include trained security personnel posted at appropriate locations to provide audience oversight and to conduct patrols, as well as state of the art night vision technology to assist in the detection of recording equipment during film presentations.

Rates for Festival 2026：Security personnel with night vision equipment — minimum standard two (2) uniformed security personnel, minimum 4 hour shift；regular hourly rate $54.00 CDN/hr. plus 13% HST；overtime hourly rate $81.00 CDN/hr plus 13% HST。Each additional uniformed security personnel：同費率，同樣 minimum 4 hour shift。

P.4 · 各場館建議最低人數與訂購程序

Minimum recommended number of anti-piracy security personnel（原文表）：

  • Visa Screening Room at the Princess of Wales Theatre — 6
  • Roy Thomson Hall — 10
  • Royal Alexandra Theatre — 6
  • Scotiabank Theatre，Screen 1, 2, 3, 4, 12, 13 & 14 — 4
  • Scotiabank Theatre，Screen 5, 6, 7, 8, 9, 10 & 11 — 2
  • TIFF Lightbox Screen 1 — 4；Screen 2, 3, 4, 5 — 2；Private Screenings 5, 6, 7 — 2

Booking Procedure：Contact Christine Morton, Operations Manager, Securitas Canada Ltd.（Office 416.774.2553 · Cell 416.989.9192 · christine.morton@securitas.ca）。Coordination of services will be directly managed by Securitas and the final invoice for services will be billed directly to the client。All bookings must be received with a minimum of forty-eight (48) hours notice；requests without 48 hours notice cannot be guaranteed and a premium (overtime) rate will be applied。If preference is for night vision not to be deployed, this request must be made at the time of booking（service pricing does not change with the elimination of night vision technology）。Rates are in effect as of June 2026 and are subject to change without notice。Valid credit card information must be provided at the time of booking.

〔按：這份表對本片有三件實事。①貼身維安大會不管——原文寫明大會不安排也不提供陪同 talent、VIP 與來賓的 close protection，要就自己找，可請 Venue Liaison 給在地合法業者名單。舒華帶保鑣一事因此是我方自理，並要在 ✉45 第 8 題「特殊安全顧慮」裡講明，讓大會與場館知道現場會多這些人。②第 9 題的「場館導覽」不是觀光——原文的 venue walk-through 由 Venue Liaison 與 Security 團隊共同帶，內容是藝人抵達、等候與離場的動線交底。首映場沒有 green room、隨行一律直接帶位（✉45），這條動線只有走一次才會清楚。③反盜錄要加購才有——大會給的是告示、映前宣導、帶位員通報這類基本措施；夜視偵測要另外付費，2026 年只有 Securitas 能做。Royal Alexandra 建議最低 6 人，依 $54 CDN/hr、最低 4 小時換算，一場約 $1,296 CDN 加 13% HST，計 $1,464.48 CDN（此為依費率表換算，不是報價單）。要訂得提前 48 小時，直接跟片方請款、訂購時要給信用卡。另：文件本身註明 confidential and not intended for wider distribution。〕
